	id = COLDWAR_IRQ_decision_events.144
	title = COLDWAR_IRQ_decision_events.144.t
	desc = COLDWAR_IRQ_decision_events.144.d
	is_triggered_only = yes
	trigger = { COLDWAR_IRQ_campaign_active = yes }
	immediate = {
		if = {
			limit = {
				NOT = { has_country_flag = COLDWAR_IRQ_D_domestic_autonomy_quoted }
				check_variable = { gdp_total > 0 }
			}
			set_variable = { COLDWAR_IRQ_D_domestic_autonomy_invoice = gdp_total }
			multiply_variable = { COLDWAR_IRQ_D_domestic_autonomy_invoice = 0.02 }

		}
	}
	option = {
		name = COLDWAR_IRQ_option_156
		ai_chance = { factor = 50 }
		trigger = { COLDWAR_IRQ_campaign_active = yes }
		if = {
			limit = { COLDWAR_IRQ_campaign_active = yes }
			if = {
				limit = {
					has_country_flag = COLDWAR_IRQ_D_domestic_autonomy_quoted
					check_variable = { COLDWAR_IRQ_D_domestic_autonomy_invoice > 0 }
					check_variable = { var = treasury value = COLDWAR_IRQ_D_domestic_autonomy_invoice compare = greater_than_or_equals }
					check_variable = { gdp_total > 0 }
					NOT = { has_country_flag = MD_skip_treasury_cost }
					OR = {
						164 = { is_owned_and_controlled_by = IRQ }
						633 = { is_owned_and_controlled_by = IRQ }
						630 = { is_owned_and_controlled_by = IRQ }
					}
					NOT = { has_country_flag = COLDWAR_IRQ_D_domestic_autonomy_used }
				}
				set_temp_variable = { treasury_change = COLDWAR_IRQ_D_domestic_autonomy_invoice }
				multiply_temp_variable = { treasury_change = -1 }
				modify_treasury_effect = yes
				set_country_flag = COLDWAR_IRQ_autonomy_accepted
				set_country_flag = COLDWAR_IRQ_autonomy_fulfilled
				add_ideas = COLDWAR_IRQ_autonomy_guarantee_cost
				COLDWAR_IRQ_reconcile_programmes = yes
				update_dirty_politicsview_expanded = yes
				set_country_flag = COLDWAR_IRQ_D_domestic_autonomy_used
			}
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = COLDWAR_IRQ_option_157
		ai_chance = { factor = 50 }
		trigger = { COLDWAR_IRQ_campaign_active = yes }
		if = {
			limit = { COLDWAR_IRQ_campaign_active = yes }
			add_stability = -0.01
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = COLDWAR_IRQ_option_158
		ai_chance = { factor = 50 }
		trigger = { has_country_flag = COLDWAR_IRQ_autonomy_accepted COLDWAR_IRQ_campaign_active = yes }
		if = {
			limit = { has_country_flag = COLDWAR_IRQ_autonomy_accepted COLDWAR_IRQ_campaign_active = yes }
			clr_country_flag = COLDWAR_IRQ_autonomy_accepted
			clr_country_flag = COLDWAR_IRQ_autonomy_fulfilled

			remove_ideas = COLDWAR_IRQ_autonomy_guarantee_cost

			update_dirty_politicsview_expanded = yes
		}

	}

							}

								check_variable = { ruling_party = 21 }

								has_country_flag = COLDWAR_IRQ_decision_foreign_credit_review_unlocked
								OR = { has_country_flag = COLDWAR_IRQ_foreign_debt_diplomacy_implemented }
							}
						}
					}
					AND = {
						COLDWAR_IRQ_campaign_active = yes
						has_country_flag = COLDWAR_IRQ_decision_foreign_credit_review_unlocked
						has_country_flag = COLDWAR_IRQ_foreign_debt_diplomacy_implemented
					}
				}
			}
			COLDWAR_IRQ_reconcile_contract_credit = yes
			set_variable = { COLDWAR_IRQ_repayment_civic_credit_offer_USA = gdp_total }
			multiply_variable = { COLDWAR_IRQ_repayment_civic_credit_offer_USA = 0.02 }
			if = {
				limit = {
					check_variable = {
						var = COLDWAR_IRQ_repayment_civic_credit_offer_USA
						value = COLDWAR_IRQ_principal_civic_credit_offer_USA
						compare = greater_than
					}
				}
				set_variable = { COLDWAR_IRQ_repayment_civic_credit_offer_USA = COLDWAR_IRQ_principal_civic_credit_offer_USA }
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					check_variable = { var = COLDWAR_IRQ_repayment_civic_credit_offer_USA value = COLDWAR_IRQ_gulf_other_debt compare = greater_than }
				}

			}

					check_variable = { var = COLDWAR_IRQ_repayment_civic_credit_offer_USA value = debt compare = greater_than }
				}
				set_variable = { COLDWAR_IRQ_repayment_civic_credit_offer_USA = debt }
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					check_variable = { var = COLDWAR_IRQ_repayment_civic_credit_offer_USA value = treasury compare = greater_than }
				}
				set_variable = { COLDWAR_IRQ_repayment_civic_credit_offer_USA = treasury }
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					check_variable = { COLDWAR_IRQ_repayment_civic_credit_offer_USA > 0 }
				}
				set_temp_variable = { COLDWAR_IRQ_gulf_other_debt_change = COLDWAR_IRQ_repayment_civic_credit_offer_USA }
				multiply_temp_variable = { COLDWAR_IRQ_gulf_other_debt_change = -1 }
				COLDWAR_IRQ_gulf_change_other_debt = yes
				set_variable = { COLDWAR_IRQ_repayment_civic_credit_offer_USA = COLDWAR_IRQ_gulf_other_debt_applied }
				multiply_variable = { COLDWAR_IRQ_repayment_civic_credit_offer_USA = -1 }
				set_temp_variable = { treasury_change = COLDWAR_IRQ_repayment_civic_credit_offer_USA }
				multiply_temp_variable = { treasury_change = -1 }
				modify_treasury_effect = yes
				subtract_from_variable = { COLDWAR_IRQ_principal_civic_credit_offer_USA = COLDWAR_IRQ_repayment_civic_credit_offer_USA }
				USA = {

					modify_treasury_effect = yes
				}
			}
